## New Tickets (Session 2026-07-08, Codex - Verified from Handoff)

- [ ] **[PLG-010]** [Build-Breaking / Missing Import] ShellIntegrationSettingsControl.xaml.cs:294 uses Task.Run without using System.Threading.Tasks. *Root Cause*: ShellIntegrationPlugin.cs has the import but ShellIntegrationSettingsControl.xaml.cs does not. Build error CS0103: Task not found in current context. ShellIntegration project never compiles; its test suite never runs. *Suggested Fix*: Add using System.Threading.Tasks; to ShellIntegrationSettingsControl.xaml.cs.

- [ ] **[PLG-011]** [Test Infrastructure / Build-Breaking] BatteryHealthServiceTests.cs:125,152 accesses task.Result after await Task.WhenAll, triggering xUnit1031 build error. *Root Cause*: After await Task.WhenAll, each individual task is already completed, so accessing .Result is redundant and violates xUnit1031. PLG solution has 5 build errors total (1 PLG-010 + 4 PLG-011). *Suggested Fix*: Replace 	ask.Result with the awaited task variable directly, or remove redundant .Result access since the task is already completed after Task.WhenAll.

_Audit log (pass-2, 2026-07-08, Codex): Filed PLG-010, PLG-011. Total open: 8 VSR + 2 PLG = 10. Next: hunt new defects across all repos._

## New Tickets (Session 2026-07-08, Codex - PLG Fresh Discoveries)

- [ ] **[PLG-012]** [Async Void / Crash Risk] PluginWorkbench MainWindow.xaml.cs has 7 unguarded async void handlers in MainWindow.xaml.cs:76,115,123,153,175,180,188. *Root Cause*: Of 9 async void WPF event handlers, only BootstrapHostButton_Click (L139) and RunOptimizationActionButton_Click (L245) have try-catch. The other 7 (MainWindow_Loaded, PluginListBox_MouseDoubleClick, LoadSelectedButton_Click, OpenZipButton_Click, ReloadCurrentButton_Click, ModeToggleButton_Click, ModeComboBox_SelectionChanged) will crash the process on unhandled exception. *Suggested Fix*: Wrap all 7 unguarded handlers in try-catch with user-facing error feedback.

_Audit log (pass-plg-1, 2026-07-08, Codex): Filed PLG-012 (PluginWorkbench async void). Total new: 8 VSR + 2 PLG (handoff) + 2 VSR + 1 PLG (fresh) = 13 open._
